- 4903 files · ~5,857,979 words

- Unclassified: 30 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 13, .css 4, .csv 3)

## Summary
- 54964 nodes · 174242 edges · 1459 communities (1142 shown, 317 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 88% EXTRACTED · 12% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 21293 edges (avg confidence: 0.94)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `b31611c3`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
